 Reactivate your EU listings - GPSR compliance required
 Hello Weihai EU,
 Some of your listings have been deactivated from our European Union (EU) stores due to missing compliance information. This action does not impact your account health.
 You can find examples of your deactivated listings at the bottom of this email, and the full list on your Account Health page. Review the affected listings and next steps to meet General Product Safety Regulation (GPSR) requirements below.
 Submit compliance information
 Submit compliance information
 How do I reactivate my listings?
 To reactivate your listings, provide the required GPSR compliance information by following the instructions below:
-
 Go to your Account Health page .
- Select 'Add filters' and check 'Listing removed' in the "Filter by action taken" column.
- Click 'Submit' next to the deactivated listing and follow the instructions provided.
- Follow the steps on Suppressed listings to resolve any outstanding listing issues. Existing issues prevent listings from being reactivated, even if you’ve fulfilled all compliance requirements.

RE:[CASE 13461408592] “无商品信息”错误
Hello from Amazon Selling Partner Support,
We see that your ASIN B0HJ268JBS is restricted from being sold due to the policy violations compliant requirement. Hence, you would like to escalate to the Restricted Products team and reinstate the ASIN.
Please be informed, we are the dedicated team to handle this issue.
We have checked and see that the ASIN: B0HJ268JBS has been restricted due to Policy Compliance as the sale of paint strippers containing Dichloromethane in violation of EC Regulation 1907/2006 is not allowed on Amazon.it. We leveraged a combination of automated means to identify this issue and to make this decision.
To submit appeal for this decision, please submit your appeal and related documents through the account health dashboard at the following link:
https://sellercentral-europe.amazon.com/performance/account/health/product-policies?t=&s=B0HJ268JBS
Once you've submitted the required documents, our dedicated team will review them. Once the review is complete, you'll receive an acknowledge email.

提交商品安全文件以重新启售商品
时间分组：today 邮箱：account_4 发件人：亚马逊开店服务 <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Fri, 6 Nov 2026 16:51:13 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:52:53
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 提交商品安全文件以重新启售商品
 尊敬的 Weihai EU：
您好！
 您的部分商品因缺少必要的商品合规信息而被停售。查看下面的详细信息和后续步骤。受影响的商品显示在此电子邮件的末尾。
 为什么会发生这种情况？
 我们之所以采取此措施，是因为这些商品缺少必要的合规信息。请查看我们的 商品安全与合规 帮助页面，了解有关适用法律法规和亚马逊政策的信息。为了确定此问题，我们综合运用了自动化技术和专家人工评审，最终做出这一决定。
 此电子邮件末尾的表格提供了特定于商品的合规性要求。
 如何重新启售商品？
 要提供所需的合规信息，请前往 账户状况 页面并找到商品合规性请求：
-
 如果您有所需的文件，请按照相关说明提交您的合规文件。我们仅会重新启售满足合规要求的商品。
-
 如果您认为您的商品无需遵守这些要求，请提交相关文件，说明它们为何不受这些合规要求的约束。
 有关分步操作说明，请参阅 提交合规文件或对文件请求提出申诉 帮助页面。
 为避免进一步影响您的账户，请停售或删除您不打算销售或申诉的任何不合规商品。
 如果我未执行上述操作，会怎么样？
 如果您未提供所需的合规信息，则受影响的 ASIN 将一直处于停售状态，直到您或其他发布此商品的销售伙伴满足合规要求。
 这些商品的亚马逊物流库存会怎样？
 您必须在收到本通知后的 30 天内从我们的运营中心移除这些商品。如果未移除，我们将按照《亚马逊服务商业解决方案协议》中的规定弃置此库存，并且费用由您承担。要了解如何移除库存，请参阅 移除库存 帮助页面。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。
 有关更多信息，请观看卖家大学中的 安全与合规简介 视频
 如果您有其他问题，请在您的 账户状况 控制面板上提交请求。
 亚马逊商品安全与合规性
 受影响的商品
 详情
 Roaxkois Töpferset für Kinder, Töpferscheibe, Töpferset für Zuhause mit ton lufttrocknend, Diamant Aufkleber, Kreativ Set für Kinder, Geschenk-Ideen, Geschenk für Mädchen Jungen
 ASIN： B0DKBX9WK8
 亚马逊要求您证明自己在亚马逊德国站上架的儿童玩具符合适用的法律、法规、标准和亚马逊政策。
要销售儿童玩具，您必须满足 儿童玩具帮助页面 上列出的要求，并与我们的合作伙伴第三方测试、检验和认证 (TIC) 服务提供商合作，验证您的商品符合这些要求。
儿童玩具是指供14岁或以下儿童在学习或玩耍时使用的商品。
亚马逊根据以下标准来确定商品是否为“儿童玩具”：
商品包装上的年龄分级标明该玩具专供 14 岁或以下儿童使用。
产品的包装、展示、促销或广告显示该产品适合 14 岁或以下儿童

We hebben gebruikgemaakt van een combinatie van geautomatiseerde middelen en deskundige menselijke beoordeling om dit probleem te identificeren en deze beslissing te nemen.

FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfillment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfillment center. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.com/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.com/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.

Hello from Amazon Selling Partner Support,

Verify your EU Responsible Person information to maintain compliance
时间分组：last7 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Europe <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Thu, 5 Nov 2026 16:52:51 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:52:42
展开查看邮件预览 
Amazon Services
 96
 Verify your EU Responsible Person information to maintain compliance
 Hello,
To keep your FR listings live in our EU stores and stay signed up for Amazon services, you must maintain a valid and up-to-date EU Responsible Person for your products. Review the details and next steps below.
 Why is this happening?
To comply with the General Product Safety Regulation, Market Surveillance Regulation, and EU Batteries Regulation, you must have a valid EU-based Responsible Person for every product you offer for sale in the EU, at all times. You must also include their contact information on your product labels and provide the information in Seller Central.
To learn more, refer to our General Product Safety Regulation (GPSR), Market Surveillance Regulation, EU Batteries Regulation - Responsible Person requirements and Submitting manufacturer, Responsible Person, and warning and safety information help pages.
 What do you need to do?
 If you are working with a third-party EU Responsible Person provider, take the following compliance steps:
 Verify your current setup
 Ensure you aren't using a provider listed on our Invalid Responsible Person providers help page, that your

提交商品安全文件以重新启售商品
时间分组：today 邮箱：account_4 发件人：亚马逊开店服务 <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Fri, 6 Nov 2026 16:51:13 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:52:53
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 提交商品安全文件以重新启售商品
 尊敬的 Weihai EU：
您好！
 您的部分商品因缺少必要的商品合规信息而被停售。查看下面的详细信息和后续步骤。受影响的商品显示在此电子邮件的末尾。
 为什么会发生这种情况？
 我们之所以采取此措施，是因为这些商品缺少必要的合规信息。请查看我们的 商品安全与合规 帮助页面，了解有关适用法律法规和亚马逊政策的信息。为了确定此问题，我们综合运用了自动化技术和专家人工评审，最终做出这一决定。
 此电子邮件末尾的表格提供了特定于商品的合规性要求。
 如何重新启售商品？
 要提供所需的合规信息，请前往 账户状况 页面并找到商品合规性请求：
-
 如果您有所需的文件，请按照相关说明提交您的合规文件。我们仅会重新启售满足合规要求的商品。
-
 如果您认为您的商品无需遵守这些要求，请提交相关文件，说明它们为何不受这些合规要求的约束。
 有关分步操作说明，请参阅 提交合规文件或对文件请求提出申诉 帮助页面。
 为避免进一步影响您的账户，请停售或删除您不打算销售或申诉的任何不合规商品。
 如果我未执行上述操作，会怎么样？
 如果您未提供所需的合规信息，则受影响的 ASIN 将一直处于停售状态，直到您或其他发布此商品的销售伙伴满足合规要求。
 这些商品的亚马逊物流库存会怎样？
 您必须在收到本通知后的 30 天内从我们的运营中心移除这些商品。如果未移除，我们将按照《亚马逊服务商业解决方案协议》中的规定弃置此库存，并且费用由您承担。要了解如何移除库存，请参阅 移除库存 帮助页面。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。
 有关更多信息，请观看卖家大学中的 安全与合规简介 视频
 如果您有其他问题，请在您的 账户状况 控制面板上提交请求。
 亚马逊商品安全与合规性
 受影响的商品
 详情
 Roaxkois Töpferset für Kinder, Töpferscheibe, Töpferset für Zuhause mit ton lufttrocknend, Diamant Aufkleber, Kreativ Set für Kinder, Geschenk-Ideen, Geschenk für Mädchen Jungen
 ASIN： B0DKBX9WK8
 亚马逊要求您证明自己在亚马逊德国站上架的儿童玩具符合适用的法律、法规、标准和亚马逊政策。
要销售儿童玩具，您必须满足 儿童玩具帮助页面 上列出的要求，并与我们的合作伙伴第三方测试、检验和认证 (TIC) 服务提供商合作，验证您的商品符合这些要求。
儿童玩具是指供14岁或以下儿童在学习或玩耍时使用的商品。
亚马逊根据以下标准来确定商品是否为“儿童玩具”：
商品包装上的年龄分级标明该玩具专供 14 岁或以下儿童使用。
产品的包装、展示、促销或广告显示该产品适合 14 岁或以下儿童

 Hello,
Your invoice for Amazon.co.uk Extended Producer Responsibility (EPR) Pay on Behalf charges is now available.
The invoice includes (1) the eco-contribution fees that Amazon paid on your behalf for sales of products subject to EPR obligations on Amazon.co.uk during the applicable reporting period, and (2) EPR Pay on Behalf service fees for the EPR categories specified in the category-level and ASIN-level summary.

 We’re adjusting rates to reflect ongoing costs, holding the FBA fuel and logistics surcharge steady, and simplifying storage fees.
 Hello,
 Last year, across stores in the US, we delivered 8 billion items same-day or next-day, and we're continuing to invest in faster speeds to help grow your business. Today, we're sharing our 2027 fee updates and the thinking behind them. We know this news arrives during your busiest season, and that fee changes, up or down, affect your planning and bottom line. We're sharing them now so that you have more than two months to plan before most changes take effect on January 15, 2027.
 On average, FBA fulfillment fees will increase by $0.19 per unit sold, approximately 0.8% of the average item’s selling price. The impact will vary by product, size, and price. Fees for some products will increase, and others will decrease, so that each fee more closely matches our cost of fulfilling that product. To see how these changes affect your business at the account and product level, go to the new Fee Change Comparison dashboard in Profit Analytics . You can also model fee scenarios with the Revenue Calculator .

 Umożliw ponowną aktywację ofert w UE – wymagana zgodność z rozporządzeniem w sprawie ogólnego bezpieczeństwa produktów
 Dzień dobry Weihai EU,
 Niektóre z Twoich ofert zostały zdezaktywowane w naszych sklepach w Unii Europejskiej (UE) z powodu braku informacji o zgodności z wymogami. Działania te nie mają wpływu na kondycję Twojego konta.
 Przykłady zdezaktywowanych ofert znajdziesz na dole tej wiadomości e-mail. Aby wyświetlić ich pełną listę, przejdź na stronę „Kondycja konta”. Aby spełnić wymogi rozporządzenia w sprawie ogólnego bezpieczeństwa produktów, zapoznaj się z poniższymi ofertami i dalszymi działaniami do wykonania.

 Jak mogę ponownie aktywować oferty produktów?

96
 Możesz teraz pobrać kwartalny raport podatkowy, który zawiera dane, takie jak informacje identyfikacyjne, liczba transakcji, przychody oraz prowizje i opłaty za usługi.
 Dzień dobry,
 Zgodnie z zasadami dotyczącymi sklepów internetowych wydanymi przez Radę Państwa Chin (dekret nr 810) oraz obwieszczeniem publicznym Państwowego Urzędu Podatkowego Chin [2025] nr 15 („PN15”) co kwartał przekazujemy chińskim organom podatkowym informacje o sprzedawcach z siedzibą w Chinach.
 Niektórzy sprzedawcy znaleźli potencjalne rozbieżności w danych zawartych w pierwszym kwartalnym raporcie podatkowym za okres od października do grudnia 2025 r. Nasz zespół dokładnie sprawdził wszystkie raporty, aby upewnić się, że prawidłowo odzwierciedlają Twoją aktywność w sklepie Amazon, i w stosownych przypadkach ponownie przesłał chińskiemu organowi podatkowemu zaktualizowane raporty za czwarty kwartał 2025 r.
 Ponownie wysyłamy Twój kwartalny raport podatkowy dla Chin. Poniższe linki będą aktywne przez siedem dni.
 Pobierz raport kwartalny
 Raport ten jest przeznaczony wyłącznie do wglądu. Nie jest wymagane żadne działanie.

 De tabel aan het einde van deze e-mail bevat productspecifieke nalevingsvereisten.

96
 Przesyłanie dokumentacji dotyczącej bezpieczeństwa produktu w celu ponownego aktywowania ofert
 Dzień dobry Weihai EU,
 niektóre z Twoich ofert zostały zdezaktywowane z powodu niespełnienia obowiązków dotyczących zgodności wystawianych przez Ciebie produktów z wymogami. Poniżej znajdziesz szczegółowe informacje oraz dalsze instrukcje. Oferty produktów, których dotyczy problem, wskazano na końcu niniejszej wiadomości e-mail.
 Dlaczego tak się stało?
 Podjęliśmy to działanie, ponieważ w przypadku tych produktów nie dostarczono obowiązkowych informacji dotyczących zgodności z wymogami. Więcej informacji na temat mających zastosowanie przepisów ustawowych i wykonawczych oraz zasad Amazon znajdziesz na stronie Bezpieczeństwo i zgodność produktu z wymogami . Do identyfikacji problemu i podjęcia decyzji wykorzystaliśmy zautomatyzowane narzędzia, a otrzymane za ich pomocą wyniki poddaliśmy weryfikacji przez eksperta.
 Informacje na temat wymogów dotyczących zgodności dla poszczególnych produktów znajdziesz w tabeli na końcu tej wiadomości e-mail.
 Jak mogę ponownie aktywować oferty?
 Aby dostarczyć niezbędne informacje dotyczące zgodności z wymogami, przejdź do panelu Kondycja konta i

-

FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfillment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfillment center. FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.

 According to rules for online stores issued by the State Council of China (Decree No. 810) and China State Taxation Administration Public Notice [2025] No.15 ("PN15"), we provide information on China-based sellers to the Chinese tax authority on a quarterly basis.

 194.13 na konto powiązane z Twoją metodą płatności (rachunek bankowy o ostatnich cyfrach 191).

 Simplified ocean shipping from China to Europe with Amazon SEND
 Now Live: Ocean shipping from China to UK & Germany with Amazon SEND
 Amazon SEND is a cross-border shipping solution available across 20 lanes, allowing sellers to book, track, and pay for shipments — all within Seller Central.
 What's new: Ocean shipping from China to Amazon FCs in the UK and Germany is now live on Amazon SEND. Choose from two partnered carriers — AAF or Jiufang — to get started.
 Transit times:
- CN → UK: Air shipping: 13-18 days | Ocean shipping: 45-58 days
- CN → DE: Air shipping: 14-18 days| Ocean shipping: 45-58 days
 Learn about Amazon SEND
 Why sellers chose Amazon SEND:
- Competitive rates: Get access to Amazon-negotiated rates when shipping to Amazon Fulfilment Centres (FCs) in the UK and Germany.
- Export now, Pay later: Charges are billed to your Seller Central account after delivery — no upfront payment. Eligible sellers also get an extended 30-day credit period.
- Documentation Support: 70% of documentation is pre-filled, reducing paperwork and speeding up your shipment creation process.
- Hassle-free customs support: End-to-end customs documentation and clearance — handled by your

 Ya puedes descargar tu declaración fiscal trimestral, que incluye datos como información de identidad, número de transacciones, ingresos y tarifas por servicios y comisiones.
 Hola:
 De acuerdo con las reglas para las tiendas en Internet emitidas por el Consejo de Estado de la República Popular China (Decreto n.° 810) y el Aviso público de la Administración Tributaria del Estado de China [2025] No.15 (“PN15”), proporcionamos trimestralmente a la autoridad fiscal china información sobre los vendedores con sede en China.
 Reenviamos tu declaración fiscal trimestral de China. Los enlaces que aparecen a continuación permanecerán activos durante siete días.
 Descargar declaración trimestral
 Esta declaración se proporciona solamente a manera de referencia, por lo que no es necesario que hagas nada.
 También te proporcionamos un reporte aun más detallado, por pedidos, y donde se muestra la información trimestral por transacción.
 Descargar reporte por pedidos
 Es normal que existan variaciones entre los datos de estos reportes, pagos y reporte de transacciones sujetas a IVA de Amazon. Cada reporte emplea una metodología distinta y cumple un propósito diferente.
 Te proporcionaremos a

 You can now download your quarterly tax report, which includes data such as identity information, number of transactions, revenue, and commission and service fees.
 Hello,
According to rules for online stores issued by the State Council of China (Decree No. 810) and China State Taxation Administration Public Notice [2025] No.15 ("PN15"), we provide information on China-based sellers to the Chinese tax authority on a quarterly basis.
Some sellers identified potential data discrepancies with the first quarterly tax report covering January to March 2026. Our team has carefully reviewed all reporting to ensure it accurately reflects your activity in our store, and resubmitted Q1 2026 reports to the Chinese tax authority where necessary.
We’re re-sending your quarterly China tax report. The links below are active for seven days.
 Download your quarterly report
This report is provided for your reference only. No action is required.
 We’ve also provided a more granular order-level report that displays your quarterly information by transaction.
 Download your order-level report
 Variation between the data in these reports and your payment and Amazon VAT transactions report is expected.

 Submit compliance information
 How do I reactivate my listings?
 To reactivate your listings, provide the required GPSR compliance information by following the instructions below:
-
 Go to your Account Health page .
- Select 'Add filters' and check 'Listing removed' in the "Filter by action taken" column.
- Click 'Submit' next to the deactivated listing and follow the instructions provided.
- Follow the steps on Suppressed listings to resolve any outstanding listing issues. Existing issues prevent listings from being reactivated, even if you’ve fulfilled all compliance requirements.

RE:[CASE 13461408592] “无商品信息”错误
Hello from Amazon Selling Partner Support,
We see that your ASIN B0HJ268JBS is restricted from being sold due to the policy violations compliant requirement. Hence, you would like to escalate to the Restricted Products team and reinstate the ASIN.
Please be informed, we are the dedicated team to handle this issue.
We have checked and see that the ASIN: B0HJ268JBS has been restricted due to Policy Compliance as the sale of paint strippers containing Dichloromethane in violation of EC Regulation 1907/2006 is not allowed on Amazon.it. We leveraged a combination of automated means to identify this issue and to make this decision.
To submit appeal for this decision, please submit your appeal and related documents through the account health dashboard at the following link:
https://sellercentral-europe.amazon.com/performance/account/health/product-policies?t=&s=B0HJ268JBS
Once you've submitted the required documents, our dedicated team will review them. Once the review is complete, you'll receive an acknowledge email.

 Why is this happening?
 Customers trust that they can always buy with confidence on Amazon. The sale of illegal, unsafe, or other restricted products is strictly prohibited.  What actions do I need to take?

 96
 Verify your EU Responsible Person information to maintain compliance
 Hello,
To keep your FR listings live in our EU stores and stay signed up for Amazon services, you must maintain a valid and up-to-date EU Responsible Person for your products. Review the details and next steps below.
 Why is this happening?
To comply with the General Product Safety Regulation, Market Surveillance Regulation, and EU Batteries Regulation, you must have a valid EU-based Responsible Person for every product you offer for sale in the EU, at all times. You must also include their contact information on your product labels and provide the information in Seller Central.
To learn more, refer to our General Product Safety Regulation (GPSR), Market Surveillance Regulation, EU Batteries Regulation - Responsible Person requirements and Submitting manufacturer, Responsible Person, and warning and safety information help pages.
 What do you need to do?
 If you are working with a third-party EU Responsible Person provider, take the following compliance steps:
 Verify your current setup
 Ensure you aren't using a provider listed on our Invalid Responsible Person providers help page, that your

时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人："gpsr-rspaduit-cn-seller-compliance@amazon.com" <gpsr-rspaduit-cn-seller-compliance@amazon.com> 日期：Thu, 29 Oct 2026 09:14:11 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:51:46

96
RE:[CASE 13434500502] 【立刻采取行动】续签您的欧盟负责人合同以避免产品被下架
【请勿回复此日志】
尊敬的卖家，
您好，
为了遵守通用产品安全法规和欧盟电池法规政策，在欧盟销售的大多数非食品消费类商品，都需要指定一名个人或公司作为商品合规性联系人（即“欧盟负责人”)。
亚马逊已收到您为产品指定的负责人【SUCCESS COURIER SL】的通知，您的某些品牌或ASIN的合同已过期。为了确保您的所有ASIN均符合通用产品安全法规和欧盟电池法规，并避免产品下架，您必须在15天内完成以下步骤：
1. 续签您与负责人服务提供商的合同，并确保所有在通用产品安全法规适用范围内的商品均受合同保护。
2. 完成上述步骤后，请务必通知您的负责人立即与亚马逊联系以确认您已续签合同。
如果您计划更换负责人，请注意，您需要为【每个】欧盟站点【分别】更新相关信息。同时，产品包装必须显示当前有效签约的欧盟负责人信息。若上传至亚马逊的安全警告信息中包含负责人信息，也需确保信息一致且有效。
如果您是品牌卖家：请前往绩效-账户状况-政策合规性-监管合规性，点击右上角的“管理您的合规性”的“提交合规信息”按钮，更新并保存受影响商品的负责人信息。
如果您是非品牌卖家：请访问商品政策合规性页面的“已存档”选项卡，更新并保存新的负责人信息。
相关页面链接：
账户状况页面： https://sellercentral.amazon.de/performance/dashboard
商品政策合规性页面: https://sellercentral.amazon.de/performance/account/health/product-policies?t=regulatory-compliance
如果您在收到此问题日志后的15天内未完成上述操作，亚马逊将移除受影响的商品信息。
要了解有关这些法规及其要求的更多信息请访问以下链接：
通用产品安全法规 (GPSR): https://sellercentral-europe.amazon.com/help/hub/reference/external/GQAYBJPNAZ2LMDDT?locale=zh-CN
欧盟电池法规 – 负责人要求: https://sellercentral.amazon.de/help/hub/reference/external/GSAVPEJBBU9WDUKA?locale=zh-CN
如果您需要寻找欧盟负责人服务商，您可以访问合规服务商城*：直接在卖家平台上查找并注册可履行欧盟负责人义务的第三方服务商。
了解更多： https://sellercentral-europe.amazon.com/service-hub?ref=email_rsprej_q3
* 服务由外部服务商提供，并受这些服

 You can now submit the required Product Safety Law information via Account Health
 Hello,
 You can now submit the required Kingdom of Saudi Arabia (KSA) Product Safety Law information via Account Health .
 Since November 14, 2024, Saudi Arabia has implemented Product Safety Law requirements that apply to most consumer products sold in Saudi Arabia, except those regulated by the Saudi Food and Drug Authority (SFDA). We recommend you submit the required information as soon as possible.
 You can provide the following information on the Account Health page:
- Legal name and contact details of the importer (if applicable)
- Legal name and contact details of the manufacturer
- Safety information, warnings, safety images or disclaimers
- Proof of your product’s compliance with applicable Saudi technical standards, such as a Product Certificate of Conformity or an alternate document
If you have an importer or a manufacturer that applies to your entire brand enrolled in Brand Registry, you can submit them at brand level:
- Go to the Regulatory Compliance section of your Account Health page.
- Click Submit compliance information , then select KSA Product Safety Law (PSL) .

 Hello Weihai EU,

 Submit product compliance documents to avoid listing removal
 Hello Weihai US,
 Your listings are at risk of removal due to missing Safety Data Sheets. Submit the required documentation by 2026-11-18 to continue selling these products. You can find examples of your at-risk listings at the bottom of this email.
 Why is this happening?
 We are taking this action because we identified hazardous products in your catalog that require Safety Data Sheets (SDS). These documents are required by law and provide critical safety information about chemical products, including proper handling, storage, and emergency procedures. This helps ensure the safety of our customers, employees, and anyone who handles these products.

提交商品安全文件以重新启售商品
时间分组：today 邮箱：account_4 发件人：亚马逊开店服务 <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Fri, 6 Nov 2026 16:51:13 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:52:53
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 提交商品安全文件以重新启售商品
 尊敬的 Weihai EU：
您好！
 您的部分商品因缺少必要的商品合规信息而被停售。查看下面的详细信息和后续步骤。受影响的商品显示在此电子邮件的末尾。
 为什么会发生这种情况？
 我们之所以采取此措施，是因为这些商品缺少必要的合规信息。请查看我们的 商品安全与合规 帮助页面，了解有关适用法律法规和亚马逊政策的信息。为了确定此问题，我们综合运用了自动化技术和专家人工评审，最终做出这一决定。
 此电子邮件末尾的表格提供了特定于商品的合规性要求。
 如何重新启售商品？
 要提供所需的合规信息，请前往 账户状况 页面并找到商品合规性请求：
-
 如果您有所需的文件，请按照相关说明提交您的合规文件。我们仅会重新启售满足合规要求的商品。
-
 如果您认为您的商品无需遵守这些要求，请提交相关文件，说明它们为何不受这些合规要求的约束。
 有关分步操作说明，请参阅 提交合规文件或对文件请求提出申诉 帮助页面。
 为避免进一步影响您的账户，请停售或删除您不打算销售或申诉的任何不合规商品。
 如果我未执行上述操作，会怎么样？
 如果您未提供所需的合规信息，则受影响的 ASIN 将一直处于停售状态，直到您或其他发布此商品的销售伙伴满足合规要求。
 这些商品的亚马逊物流库存会怎样？
 您必须在收到本通知后的 30 天内从我们的运营中心移除这些商品。如果未移除，我们将按照《亚马逊服务商业解决方案协议》中的规定弃置此库存，并且费用由您承担。要了解如何移除库存，请参阅 移除库存 帮助页面。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。
 有关更多信息，请观看卖家大学中的 安全与合规简介 视频
 如果您有其他问题，请在您的 账户状况 控制面板上提交请求。

 We’re adjusting rates to reflect ongoing costs, holding the FBA fuel and logistics surcharge steady, and simplifying storage fees.
 Hello,
 Last year, across stores in the US, we delivered 8 billion items same-day or next-day, and we're continuing to invest in faster speeds to help grow your business. Today, we're sharing our 2027 fee updates and the thinking behind them. We know this news arrives during your busiest season, and that fee changes, up or down, affect your planning and bottom line. We're sharing them now so that you have more than two months to plan before most changes take effect on January 15, 2027.
 On average, FBA fulfillment fees will increase by $0.19 per unit sold, approximately 0.8% of the average item’s selling price. The impact will vary by product, size, and price. Fees for some products will increase, and others will decrease, so that each fee more closely matches our cost of fulfilling that product. To see how these changes affect your business at the account and product level, go to the new Fee Change Comparison dashboard in Profit Analytics . You can also model fee scenarios with the Revenue Calculator .

 Verifiera din information om ansvarig person i EU för att säkerställa överensstämmelse
 Hej!
För att hålla dina produktposter på SE aktiva i våra EU-butiker och fortsätta vara registrerad för Amazon-tjänster måste du ha en giltig och uppdaterad ansvarig person i EU för dina produkter. Gå igenom informationen och följande steg nedan.
 Varför händer det här?
För att följa förordningen om allmän produktsäkerhet, förordningen om marknadskontroll och EU:s batteriförordning måste du alltid ha en giltig ansvarig person baserad i EU för varje produkt du säljer i EU. Du måste också ange denna persons kontaktuppgifter på dina produktetiketter och ange informationen i Seller Central.
Mer information finns på våra hjälpsidor Förordningen om allmän produktsäkerhet (GPSR), Förordningen om marknadskontroll, EU:s batteriförordning – krav på ansvarig person samt Skicka in uppgifter om tillverkare, ansvarig person samt varnings- och säkerhetsinformation .
 Vad behöver du göra?
 Om du samarbetar med en tredjepartsleverantör av ansvariga personer i EU ska du vidta följande åtgärder för överensstämmelse:
 Verifiera din nuvarande situation
 Se till att du inte använder en leverantör

 Dangerous goods (hazmat) status update
 Hello from Fulfillment by Amazon,
 Amazon performs regular reviews on products in our fulfillment centers that may be regulated as dangerous goods (also known as hazmat). We conduct these reviews to help ensure that our shipments meet regulatory requirements and safety standards for our customers and employees. Following one such review, the dangerous goods status of one or more of your products has changed. Please read the information below to learn more about what this means for your existing inventory and new shipments.

 Dzień dobry,

Nasz zespół dokładnie sprawdził wszystkie raporty, aby upewnić się, że prawidłowo odzwierciedlają Twoją aktywność w sklepie Amazon, i w stosownych przypadkach ponownie przesłał chińskiemu organowi podatkowemu zaktualizowane raporty za czwarty kwartał 2025 r.
 Ponownie wysyłamy Twój kwartalny raport podatkowy dla Chin. Poniższe linki będą aktywne przez siedem dni.

 Przesyłanie dokumentacji dotyczącej bezpieczeństwa produktu w celu ponownego aktywowania ofert
 Dzień dobry Weihai EU,
 niektóre z Twoich ofert zostały zdezaktywowane z powodu niespełnienia obowiązków dotyczących zgodności wystawianych przez Ciebie produktów z wymogami. Poniżej znajdziesz szczegółowe informacje oraz dalsze instrukcje. Oferty produktów, których dotyczy problem, wskazano na końcu niniejszej wiadomości e-mail.
 Dlaczego tak się stało?
 Podjęliśmy to działanie, ponieważ w przypadku tych produktów nie dostarczono obowiązkowych informacji dotyczących zgodności z wymogami. Więcej informacji na temat mających zastosowanie przepisów ustawowych i wykonawczych oraz zasad Amazon znajdziesz na stronie Bezpieczeństwo i zgodność produktu z wymogami . Do identyfikacji problemu i podjęcia decyzji wykorzystaliśmy zautomatyzowane narzędzia, a otrzymane za ich pomocą wyniki poddaliśmy weryfikacji przez eksperta.
 Informacje na temat wymogów dotyczących zgodności dla poszczególnych produktów znajdziesz w tabeli na końcu tej wiadomości e-mail.
 Jak mogę ponownie aktywować oferty?

New EU customs handling fee from Nov 1
 96
 The EU will introduce a €2 charge for all imports shipped directly from outside the EU to customers in the EU, as of Nov 1.

Temporary peak season extensions for international FBA shipments
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：The Fulfillment by Amazon team <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Fri, 30 Oct 2026 16:16:02 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:51:56
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 Review the updated the abandoned shipment and automatic shipment closure windows for international shipments.
 Hello,
We know many of you are experiencing longer ocean transit times, port delays, and customs holds this peak season due to external factors beyond your control.
To give you more flexibility, we’ve made two temporary changes for international shipment plans created between August 1 and November 30, 2026:
- We’ve extended the window for related shipments to arrive after the first shipment in a multi-destination plan is received from 30 to 45 days. You won’t be charged the deleted or abandoned defect fee as long as your remaining shipments are received within 45 days of the first receive.
- We’ve extended the automatic shipment closure window to 90 days , giving your in-transit shipments more time to arrive before they close. What to expect:
- The defect will still be flagged on your Inbound Performance Dashboard but the fee is waived for eligible shipments (defined above).
- Any inbound defect fees already charged on eligible shipments will be reimbursed. Go to your Reimbursement report to view details. No action is required. These changes and reimbursements will be

 Hello,
You're incurring elevated storage fees because some of your ASINs have excess inventory. To identify these ASINs, go to FBA Inventory , and review the Estimated excess units column.
We recommend that you select one or more of the following options to help improve your unit sales and reduce these storage fees:
-
 Create a deal : Deals can help drive more traffic to your products and sell excess inventory, because customers are often looking for discounts and savings on the items that they want to buy.
-
 Create a coupon : Coupons, which are similar to deals but have fewer discounting requirement, can also help promote your products. Coupons display in search results, on product detail pages, and in carts.
-
 Create a Sponsored Products ad : Sponsored Products is a cost-per-click advertising solution that provides you with controls and visibility to help get your products discovered. These ads help generate sales from traffic sources in the Amazon store, as well as on select premium apps, such as Prime Video, and websites.
For more information, go to Manage excess inventory .
Thank you,

Prepare now for U.S. regulatory import changes
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：The Amazon Services team <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Mon, 19 Oct 2026 17:21:18 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:51:22
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 Upcoming customs changes may affect your U.S. imports. Check your Importer of Record status and review your options today.
 Hello,
 As we previously communicated, the U.S. government is currently working to implement Executive Order 14411 on Strengthening Customs Enforcement. The executive order directs government agencies to develop new rules for U.S. imports. Now, a new rule for foreign importers of record is under government review and could be implemented quickly.
 While nothing changes for you today and your current import operations continue as usual, we strongly recommend that you take the time to review your customs arrangements and prepare now.
 What’s changing?
The executive order proposes different requirements depending on how an Importer of Record (IOR) is classified. Your IOR — the person or company officially responsible for importing your goods into the United States — determines how you may be affected. If your IOR is classified as a foreign entity under the future rules, additional customs requirements could apply. If your IOR is US-based, you will likely face fewer changes than sellers using a foreign-based IOR.
 How to prepare
- Confirm your IOR status. If y

Update to FBA and MCF EU fuel and logistics-related surcharge
 96
                                                     ‍͏  ͏  ‍͏  ͏  ‍͏  ͏  ‍͏  ͏  ‍͏  ͏  ‍͏  ͏
 Update to FBA and MCF EU fuel and logistics-related surcharge
 Hello,
 Elevated costs in fuel and logistics have continued to increase across the industry, and we’ve absorbed these costs where possible. In April 2026, we introduced a surcharge on our fulfillment fees to recover a portion of these elevated costs, similar to other major carriers. Due to these continued increases, we’re updating the surcharge.
 Starting November 2, 2026, the fuel and logistics-related surcharge will increase from 1.5% to 2% and will apply to fulfillment fees across Fulfillment by Amazon (FBA) in the UK, France, Germany, Italy, Spain, Poland, Sweden, Netherlands, Ireland, and Belgium. On November 19, 2026, this same increase will take effect for Multi-Channel Fulfillment (MCF) in the UK, France, Germany, Italy, and Spain.
 The surcharge will continue to be calculated on your fulfillment fees, not on the sale price of your items. On average, this equates to £0.02/€0.02 increase per unit for EU FBA, though this will vary based on your item’s size

提交商品安全文件以避免商品被停售
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：亚马逊开店服务 <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Thu, 15 Oct 2026 14:24:32 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:51:09
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 尊敬的 Weihai EU：
您好！
 您的部分商品因缺少必要的商品合规信息而面临被停售的风险。查看下面的详细信息和后续步骤。受影响的商品显示在此电子邮件的末尾。
 为什么会发生这种情况？
 我们之所以采取此措施，是因为这些商品缺少必要的合规信息。请查看我们的 商品安全与合规 帮助页面，了解有关适用法律法规和亚马逊政策的信息。
 此电子邮件末尾的表格提供了特定于商品的合规性要求。
 如何重新启售商品？
 要提供所需的合规信息，请前往 账户状况 页面并找到商品合规性请求：
-
 如果您有所需的文件，请按照相关说明提交您的合规文件。
-
 如果您认为您的商品无需遵守这些要求，请提交相关文件，说明它们为何不受这些合规要求的约束。
 有关分步操作说明，请参阅 提交合规文件或对文件请求提出申诉 帮助页面。
 为避免进一步影响您的账户，请停售或删除您不打算销售或申诉的任何不合规商品。
 如果我未执行上述操作，会怎么样？
 如果您未在“账户状况”页面显示的截止日期之前提供所需的合规信息，我们可能会停售受影响的商品。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。
 有关更多信息，请观看卖家大学中的 安全与合规简介 视频
 如果您有其他问题，请在您的 账户状况 控制面板上提交请求。
 亚马逊商品安全与合规性
 受影响的商品
 详情
 3-in-1 RC Excavator Toy for Kids Ages 6–12, 2.4 GHz Remote-Controlled Excavator with Metal Attachment, Tracked Vehicle with 680° Rotation for Sandboxes & Outdoor Use, Gift for Boys & Girls
 ASIN： B0HF12HY1N
 亚马逊要求您表明您在爱尔兰亚马逊上发布的儿童玩具遵守相关法律、法规、标准和亚马逊政策。
要销售儿童玩具，您必须满足 儿童玩具“帮助”页面 上列出的要求，并与我们的合作伙伴第三方测试、检验和认证（TIC）服务提供商合作，验证您的商品符合这些要求。
儿童玩具是指供14岁或以下儿童在学习或玩耍时使用的商品。
亚马逊根据以下标准来确定商品是否为“儿童玩具”：
商品包装上的年龄分级标明该玩具适合 14 岁或以下儿童使用。
商品的包装、展示、促销或广告显示该商品适合 14 岁或以下儿童使用。
消费者通常认为该商品供 14 岁或以下儿童使用。
有关合规要求、如何与 TIC 服务提供商合作的更多信息，或者要观看有关本政策涵盖的儿童玩具的视频，请访问 儿童玩具“帮助”页面 。

 À compter du 7 octobre, nous mettrons en réserve les frais du service REP : Nous payons en votre nom afin d’assurer des paiements rapides aux organisations environnementales.
 Bonjour,
 À compter du 7 octobre 2026, nous mettrons en réserve les frais d’éco-contribution et les frais du service Responsabilité élargie des producteurs : Nous payons en votre nom (REP) sur votre compte vendeur pour vos ventes réalisées sur notre site de vente amazon.com.be.
 Le service REP : Nous payons en votre nom calcule et paie les frais d’éco-contribution pour vos ventes et débite ensuite ces montants de votre compte. Une fois les frais facturés, les réserves excédentaires sont reversées sur votre compte.
 Pour commencer, le montant en réserve inclura vos frais de service annuels (fixes 25 €), ainsi que tous les frais éco-contribution en attente. Après la première réserve, les réserves mensuelles seront basées uniquement sur vos frais d’éco-contribution issus des ventes mensuelles.
Par exemple, le premier mois, si vous êtes inscrit dans une catégorie REP (par exemple, les emballages) et que vous disposez de 100 € de frais d’éco-contribution en attente, nous réservons 125 € (frais

 Bonjour Weihai EU,

 Soumettre des informations de conformité
 Soumettre des informations de conformité
 Comment réactiver mes produits mis en vente ?
 Pour réactiver vos mises en vente, fournissez les informations de conformité pour la RSGP requises en suivant les instructions ci-dessous :
-
 Accédez à votre page État du compte .
- Cliquez sur « Ajouter des filtres » et cochez « Mise en vente supprimée » dans la colonne « Filtrer par action prise ».
- Cliquez sur « Soumettre » à côté du produit mis en vente désactivé et suivez les instructions fournies.
- Suivez les étapes de la section Mise en vente supprimée

 Activeer je EU-productvermeldingen opnieuw - VAPV-naleving vereist
 Hallo Weihai EU,
 Sommige van je productvermeldingen zijn gedeactiveerd in onze stores van de Europese Unie (EU) omdat er nalevingsinformatie ontbreekt. Deze actie heeft geen invloed op je accountstatus.
 Onderaan deze e-mail vind je voorbeelden van je gedeactiveerde productvermeldingen. De volledige lijst staat op je pagina Accountstatus. Bekijk hieronder de betroffen productvermeldingen en de stappen die je moet volgen om te voldoen aan de vereisten van de Verordening algemene productveiligheid (VAPV).
 Nalevingsinformatie indienen
 Nalevingsinformatie indienen
 Hoe activeer ik mijn productvermeldingen opnieuw?
 Als je je productvermeldingen opnieuw wilt activeren, moet je de vereiste nalevingsinformatie met betrekking tot de VAPV verstrekken door de onderstaande instructies te volgen:
-
 Ga naar je pagina Accountstatus .
- Selecteer ‘Filters toevoegen’ en vink ‘Verwijderde productvermelding’ aan in de kolom ‘Filteren op ondernomen actie’.
- Klik op ‘Indienen’ naast de gedeactiveerde productvermelding en volg de instructies.
- Volg de stappen op Onderdrukte productvermeldingen om openstaande problemen met de

时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：亚马逊开店服务 <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Sun, 11 Oct 2026 14:11:40 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-11-06 19:50:46

 尊敬的 Weihai EU：
您好！
 您的部分商品因缺少必要的商品合规信息而面临被停售的风险。查看下面的详细信息和后续步骤。受影响的商品显示在此电子邮件的末尾。
 为什么会发生这种情况？
 我们之所以采取此措施，是因为这些商品缺少必要的合规信息。请查看我们的 商品安全与合规 帮助页面，了解有关适用法律法规和亚马逊政策的信息。
 此电子邮件末尾的表格提供了特定于商品的合规性要求。
 如何重新启售商品？
 要提供所需的合规信息，请前往 账户状况 页面并找到商品合规性请求：
-
 如果您有所需的文件，请按照相关说明提交您的合规文件。
-
 如果您认为您的商品无需遵守这些要求，请提交相关文件，说明它们为何不受这些合规要求的约束。
 有关分步操作说明，请参阅 提交合规文件或对文件请求提出申诉 帮助页面。
 为避免进一步影响您的账户，请停售或删除您不打算销售或申诉的任何不合规商品。
 如果我未执行上述操作，会怎么样？
 如果您未在“账户状况”页面显示的截止日期之前提供所需的合规信息，我们可能会停售受影响的商品。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。
 有关更多信息，请观看卖家大学中的 安全与合规简介 视频
 如果您有其他问题，请在您的 账户状况 控制面板上提交请求。

96
 Przesyłanie dokumentacji dotyczącej bezpieczeństwa produktu w celu ponownego aktywowania ofert
 Dzień dobry Weihai EU,
 niektóre z Twoich ofert zostały zdezaktywowane z powodu niespełnienia obowiązków dotyczących zgodności wystawianych przez Ciebie produktów z wymogami. Poniżej znajdziesz szczegółowe informacje oraz dalsze instrukcje. Oferty produktów, których dotyczy problem, wskazano na końcu niniejszej wiadomości e-mail.
 Dlaczego tak się stało?
 Podjęliśmy to działanie, ponieważ w przypadku tych produktów nie dostarczono obowiązkowych informacji dotyczących zgodności z wymogami. Więcej informacji na temat mających zastosowanie przepisów ustawowych i wykonawczych oraz zasad Amazon znajdziesz na stronie Bezpieczeństwo i zgodność produktu z wymogami . Do identyfikacji problemu i podjęcia decyzji wykorzystaliśmy zautomatyzowane narzędzia, a otrzymane za ich pomocą wyniki poddaliśmy weryfikacji przez eksperta.
 Informacje na temat wymogów dotyczących zgodności dla poszczególnych produktów znajdziesz w tabeli na końcu tej wiadomości e-mail.
 Jak mogę ponownie aktywować oferty?
 Aby dostarczyć niezbędne informacje dotyczące zgodności z wymogami, przejdź do panelu Kondycja konta i

We leveraged a combination of automated means to identify this issue and make this decision.
 How do I address this?
Follow these steps to manage your unsellable inventory:
- Check your complete list and quantity of unsellable items in the Manage FBA inventory .
- Create a removal order (removal fees apply) by following the instructions in Remove inventory overview .
 To avoid future manual inventory removal orders, set up Automated Unfulfillab

 When you click “Get help and resources,” you’ll be directed to Seller Assistant – our AI-powered assistant.
 Hello,
 Today, I want to share an important update on how we support you in Seller Central that’s designed to help you resolve issues faster. The Get help and resources page is becoming Seller Assistant, our AI-powered assistant for sellers.
Here’s what you can expect:
- We fix issues on the spot. Seller Assistant uses your account details and the information you provide to resolve common issues right away. In beta, many of these issues were resolved in seconds instead of requiring a support case.
- When you need a person, you get a person. Seller Assistant collects the details up front, assembles your case and hands it off to an associate who already knows what’s going on, so you don’t have to start over. We’re rolling this out over the next two months. Although early results are promising, we know it won’t be perfect at the start and we want to get it right. As we roll this out, my team and I will be listening closely to your feedback and making improvements along the way. If something isn’t working, we’ll fix it.

 Envie os documentos de conformidade do produto para evitar a remoção da oferta
 Olá, Weihai US.
 Suas ofertas correm o risco de serem removidas devido à falta de Fichas de Informações de Segurança de Produtos Químicos. Envie a documentação necessária até 2026-11-18 para continuar vendendo esses produtos. Você pode encontrar exemplos de ofertas de produtos em risco na parte inferior deste e-mail.
 Por que isso está acontecendo?
 Estamos realizando tomando essa medida porque identificamos produtos perigosos em seu catálogo que exigem Fichas de Informações de Segurança de Produtos Químicos. Esses documentos são exigidos por lei e fornecem informações de segurança importantes sobre produtos químicos, incluindo procedimentos apropriados de manuseio, armazenamento e emergência. Isso ajuda a garantir a segurança de nossos clientes, funcionários e qualquer pessoa que venha a manusear esses produtos.
 Para saber mais, acesse nossos Requisitos e perguntas frequentes sobre a venda de produtos químicos .
 Como faço para continuar vendendo os produtos afetados?
 Para evitar a remoção da oferta e continuar vendendo esses produtos, faça upload das Fichas de Informações de Segurança de Produto

 New badge launches in two weeks - set your Business Price at least 5% below standard retail price to get started
 Earn the Business Discount badge and savings %
 A new "Business Discount" badge will be available on Amazon Business in the next two weeks. When your single-unit Business Price offers at least 5% savings against a validated reference price (VRP), your offer automatically earns the badge and displays strikethrough price with savings percentage (%). The badge is featured across search results and detail pages, reaching millions of organizations buying on Amazon Business.
 In 2026, B2B sales of products with Business Pricing grew nearly 2x the growth rate compared to those without.¹ The badge, strikethrough price, and savings % can help you stand out to business buyers who consolidate spend with competitive sellers. Think of it like a deal badge, but always-on for your qualifying B2B offers.
 Set Business Price
